      <div class="history" id="stat.115A.1415.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/114/#laws.4.78.0">2013 c 114 art 4 s 78</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/1/4/#laws.4.107.0">1Sp2015 c 4 art 4 s 107</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/60/#laws.3.8.0">2023 c 60 art 3 s 8</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115A.1416">
        <h1 class="shn">115A.1416 BOAT WRAP PRODUCT STEWARDSHIP PROGRAM.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1416.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Definitions.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For the purposes of this section, the following terms have the meanings given.</p>
          <p>(b) "Boat" has the meaning given to watercraft under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/86B.005#stat.86B.005.18">86B.005, subdivision 18</a>.</p>
          <p>(c) "Boat wrap" means plastic that is used, intended for use, designed, or marketed for the purposes of wrapping a boat to protect it against moisture and damage from other potentially harmful elements during storage.</p>
          <p>(d) "Brand" means a name, symbol, word, or mark that identifies boat wrap and attributes it to the boat wrap producer.</p>
          <p>(e) "Independent auditor" means an independent and actively licensed certified public accountant that is:</p>
          <p>(1) retained by a stewardship organization;</p>
          <p>(2) not otherwise employed by or affiliated with the stewardship organization; and</p>
          <p>(3) qualified to conduct the audit required under subdivision 16.</p>
          <p>(f) "Producer" means, with respect to boat wrap that is sold, offered for sale, imported, or distributed in the state by any means, a person that:</p>
          <p>(1) manufactured the boat wrap under a brand that the person owns or controls;</p>
          <p>(2) owns or controls or is licensed to use a brand for boat wrap;</p>
          <p>(3) imported or imports the boat wrap into the United States; or</p>
          <p>(4) distributed or distributes boat wrap in or into the state.</p>
          <p>(g) "Recycle" or "recycling" means the process of transforming boat wrap through mechanical processes into a finished product for use or into a new material capable of being processed into a finished product. Recycle or recycling does not include:</p>
          <p>(1) altering the chemical structure of boat wrap;</p>
          <p>(2) using boat wrap as or processing boat wrap into a feedstock to produce transportation fuels; or</p>
          <p>(3) destroying boat wrap by incineration or other processes.</p>
          <p>(h) "Retailer" means a person that sells or offers boat wrap for sale in or into this state by any means.</p>
          <p>(i) "Stewardship organization" means an organization designated by one or more producers to act on their behalf as an agent to design, submit, and implement a product stewardship plan under this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1416.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Product stewardship program.</span></h2>
          <p>A producer selling or offering boat wrap for sale in or into this state must, through membership in a stewardship organization, implement and finance a statewide product stewardship program according to a stewardship plan approved by the commissioner to reduce the volume of boat wrap disposed of in landfills by promoting and providing for the negotiation and execution of agreements to collect, transport, reuse, and recycle boat wrap.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1416.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Participation required to sell.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) On and after September 1, 2025, no person may use boat wrap, sell boat wrap, or offer boat wrap for sale in or into this state unless the producer participates in an approved stewardship plan through a stewardship organization.</p>
          <p>(b) Each producer must enter into an agreement with a stewardship organization to operate, on the producer's behalf, a product stewardship program approved by the commissioner.</p>
          <p>(c) All producers offering boat wrap for sale in or into this state must become a member of a single stewardship organization implementing a single stewardship plan.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1416.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Stewardship plan required.</span></h2>
          <p>On or before March 1, 2025, a stewardship organization, on behalf of member producers, must submit a stewardship plan to the commissioner for review and approval or rejection. A stewardship plan must include all elements required under subdivision 5.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1416.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Plan content.</span></h2>
          <p>A stewardship plan must contain:</p>
          <p>(1) contact information for the individual and the entity submitting the plan, a list of all producers participating in the product stewardship program, and the brands of boat wrap included in the product stewardship program;</p>
          <p>(2) certification that the product stewardship program will accept all discarded boat wrap regardless of who produced it;</p>
          <p>(3) a description of methods by which boat wrap will be collected in all areas of the state in compliance with subdivision 14, including:</p>
          <p>(i) an explanation of how the collection system will be convenient and adequate to serve the needs of boat owners, marinas, and boat storage establishments in both urban and rural areas on an ongoing basis; and</p>
          <p>(ii) a discussion of how existing marinas, boat storage establishments, and sites designated as recycling centers under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.555">115A.555</a> will be considered when selecting collection sites;</p>
          <p>(4) a description of how the performance of the collection and recycling program will be measured, monitored, and maintained;</p>
          <p>(5) the names and locations of collectors, transporters, reuse facilities, and recyclers that will manage discarded boat wrap;</p>
          <p>(6) a description of how discarded boat wrap will be safely and securely transported, tracked, and handled from collection through final recycling and disposal of residuals;</p>
          <p>(7) a description of the methods that will be used to separate and manage nonrecyclable materials attached to boat wrap and to recycle discarded boat wrap;</p>
          <p>(8) a description of the promotion and outreach activities that will be undertaken to encourage participation in the boat wrap collection and recycling programs and how their effectiveness will be evaluated;</p>
          <p>(9) the annual performance goals established by the commissioner under subdivision 12;</p>
          <p>(10) evidence of adequate insurance and financial assurance that may be required for collection, transport, reuse, recycling, and disposal operations; and</p>
          <p>(11) a discussion of the status of end markets for collected boat wrap and what, if any, additional end markets are needed to improve the functioning of the program.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1416.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Consultation required.</span></h2>
          <p>In developing a stewardship plan, a stewardship organization must consult with stakeholders, including boat owners, owners of marinas and boat storage establishments, contractors, collectors, recyclers, Tribes, and local government units.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1416.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Agency review and approval or rejection.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Within 120 days after receiving a proposed stewardship plan, the commissioner must determine whether the plan complies with subdivision 5. If the commissioner approves a plan, the commissioner must notify the applicant of the plan approval in writing. If the commissioner rejects a plan, the commissioner must notify the applicant in writing of the reasons for rejection. An applicant whose plan is rejected by the commissioner must submit a revised plan to the commissioner within 60 days after receiving notice of rejection. If a revised plan is rejected by the commissioner, the commissioner may elect to write a plan that the applicant must implement.</p>
          <p>(b) Commissioner approval of a written plan amendment is required before a stewardship organization may make any change to an approved plan or its implementation. A proposed plan amendment must be submitted to the commissioner for review and approval or rejection according to paragraph (a) and subdivision 8.</p>
          <p>(c) A stewardship organization may operate under an approved stewardship plan for five years after the date the plan is approved by the commissioner, at which time the plan expires.</p>
          <p>(d) Six months before an approved stewardship plan expires, a stewardship organization must submit a new proposed stewardship plan to the commissioner that meets the requirements of this section. The commissioner must review and approve or reject the new proposed stewardship plan according to this subdivision and subdivision 8.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1416.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Plan availability.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner must make a proposed stewardship plan or proposed plan amendment available on the agency website for public review and comment at least 45 days before the commissioner's decision regarding plan approval or rejection. The commissioner must make an approved stewardship plan available on the agency website.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1416.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Conduct authorized.</span></h2>
          <p>A stewardship organization that organizes collection, transport, reuse, and recycling of boat wrap under this section is immune from liability for conduct under state laws relating to antitrust, restraint of trade, unfair trade practices, and other regulation of trade or commerce only to the extent that the conduct is necessary to plan and implement the producer's or organization's chosen collection, transportation, reuse, or recycling program.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1416.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Stewardship organization responsibilities.</span></h2>
          <p>A stewardship organization must provide boat wrap purchasers with educational materials regarding the product stewardship program. The materials must include, but are not limited to, information regarding available collection, transportation, reuse, and recycling options for boat wrap offered through the product stewardship program.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1416.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Retailer responsibilities.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A retailer and a wholesaler are responsible for reviewing the list of compliant producers on the agency website, maintained under subdivision 12, to determine whether a producer is compliant with this section.</p>
          <p>(b) A retailer or wholesaler of boat wrap is not in violation of this subdivision if, on the date the boat wrap was ordered from a producer or wholesaler, the producer was listed as compliant on the agency website.</p>
          <p>(c) A retailer may elect to participate as a designated point where boat wrap is collected as part of a product stewardship program approved under this section and in accordance with applicable law.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1416.12">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.<span class="headnote">Agency responsibilities.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner must maintain on the agency website a list of all compliant producers and brands participating in a stewardship plan that the commissioner has approved and a list of all producers and brands the commissioner has identified as noncompliant with this section.</p>
          <p>(b) The commissioner must, in consultation with the stewardship organization, establish annual performance goals regarding the percentage and weight of boat wrap collected and recycled that the stewardship organization must incorporate into its stewardship plan and meet annually. The performance goals must increase each year and be based on:</p>
          <p>(1) the most recent collection data available for the state;</p>
          <p>(2) the estimated weight of boat wrap sold and discarded annually;</p>
          <p>(3) actual collection data from boat wrap recycling or stewardship programs operating in other states; and</p>
          <p>(4) continuous progress necessary to meet the requirements in paragraph (c).</p>
          <p>(c) By June 1, 2030, no less than 50 percent of the total weight of boat wrap sold in this state must be collected and recycled. By June 1, 2035, no less than 80 percent of the total weight of boat wrap sold in this state must be collected and recycled.</p>
          <p>(d) After June 1, 2035, the commissioner may establish additional requirements for the percentage of boat wrap sold in the state that must be collected and recycled. The requirements must not be less than those listed in this subdivision and must be based on the factors in paragraph (b), clauses (1) to (3).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1416.13">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13.<span class="headnote">Administrative fee.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A stewardship organization must pay an annual administrative fee to the commissioner. Before June 1, 2025, and before each June 1 thereafter, the commissioner must identify the costs the agency incurs to administer and enforce this section. The commissioner must set the fee at an amount that, when paid by the stewardship organization, is sufficient to reimburse the agency's full costs of administering and enforcing this section but does not exceed those costs.</p>
          <p>(b) A stewardship organization must pay the administrative fee required under this subdivision on or before July 1, 2025, and annually thereafter, on a schedule and in a manner prescribed by the commissioner.</p>
          <p>(c) The commissioner must deposit all fees received under this subdivision in the account established in subdivision 15.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1416.14">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 14.<span class="headnote">User fees prohibited.</span></h2>
          <p>The stewardship program must be fully paid for by producers, without any fee, charge, surcharge, or any other cost to members of the public, businesses other than a producer, persons managing boat wrap, the state or any political subdivision, or any other person who is not a producer.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1416.15">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 15.<span class="headnote">Account established.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A boat wrap stewardship account is established in the special revenue fund in the state treasury. The account consists of money received from the administrative fee established in subdivision 13. The commissioner must manage the account.</p>
          <p>(b) Money in the account is appropriated annually to the commissioner for administering and enforcing this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1416.16">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 16.<span class="headnote">Stewardship reports.</span></h2>
          <p>Beginning March 1, 2026, and each March 1 thereafter, a stewardship organization operating under this section must submit an annual report to the commissioner describing the program operations of the stewardship plan during the previous calendar year. At a minimum, the report must contain:</p>
          <p>(1) a description of the methods used to collect, transport, reuse, and recycle discarded boat wrap in all regions of the state;</p>
          <p>(2) the weight of all boat wrap collected and recycled in each separate region of the state;</p>
          <p>(3) the weight of all boat wrap sold in the state;</p>
          <p>(4) the weight of discarded boat wrap collected in the state by method of disposition, including recycling, reuse, disposal of residuals, and other methods of processing;</p>
          <p>(5) a comparison of the amount of boat wrap collected and recycled with the performance goals established according to subdivision 12 and, if the goals have not been met, a discussion of why the performance goals were not met and proposed modifications to the collection program the stewardship organization will implement to ensure that future performance goals will be met;</p>
          <p>(6) samples of educational materials provided to boat wrap consumers, marinas, and boat storage establishments and an evaluation of the effectiveness of the materials and the methods used to disseminate the materials; and</p>
          <p>(7) an independent financial audit of stewardship organization activities performed by an independent auditor. The independent auditor must be selected by the stewardship organization and approved or rejected by the commissioner. If the commissioner rejects an independent auditor, the operator must select a different independent auditor for approval or rejection by the commissioner. The independent audit must meet the requirements of Accounting Standards Update 2018-08, Not-for-Profit Entities (Topic 958), Financial Accounting Standards Board, as amended.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1416.17">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 17.<span class="headnote">Data classification.</span></h2>
          <p>Trade secret and sales information, as defined under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/13.37">13.37</a>, submitted to the commissioner under this section are private or nonpublic data under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/13.37">13.37</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1416.18">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 18.<span class="headnote">Duty to provide information.</span></h2>
          <p>Upon request of the commissioner for purposes of determining compliance with this section, a person must furnish to the commissioner any information that the person has or may reasonably obtain.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115A.1416.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/116/#laws.2.9.0">2024 c 116 art 2 s 9</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115A.142">
        <h1 class="shn">115A.142 REPORT TO LEGISLATURE AND GOVERNOR.</h1>
        <p>As part of the report required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.121">115A.121</a>, the commissioner of the Pollution Control Agency shall provide a report to the governor and the legislature on the implementation of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.1415">115A.1415</a>.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115A.142.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/114/#laws.4.79.0">2013 c 114 art 4 s 79</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115A.144">
        <h1 class="shn">115A.144 SHORT TITLE.</h1>
        <p>Sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.144">115A.144</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.1463">115A.1463</a> may be cited as the "Packaging Waste and Cost Reduction Act."</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115A.144.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/116/#laws.5.1.0">2024 c 116 art 5 s 1</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115A.1441">
        <h1 class="shn">115A.1441 DEFINITIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1441.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Scope.</span></h2>
          <p>For the purposes of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.144">115A.144</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.1463">115A.1463</a>, the terms in this section have the meanings given.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1441.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Advisory board.</span></h2>
          <p>"Advisory board" or "board" means the Producer Responsibility Advisory Board established under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.1444">115A.1444</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1441.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Brand.</span></h2>
          <p>"Brand" means a name, symbol, word, or mark that identifies a product and attributes the product and its components, including packaging, to the brand owner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1441.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Brand owner.</span></h2>
          <p>"Brand owner" means a person that owns or licenses a brand or that otherwise has rights to market a product under the brand, whether or not the brand's trademark is registered.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1441.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Collection rate.</span></h2>
          <p>"Collection rate" means the amount of a covered material by covered materials type collected by service providers and transported for recycling or composting divided by the total amount of the type of a covered material by covered materials type sold or distributed into the state by the relevant unit of measurement established in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.1451">115A.1451</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1441.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Compostable material.</span></h2>
          <p>"Compostable material" means a covered material that:</p>
          <p>(1) meets, and is labeled to reflect that it meets, the American Society for Testing and Materials Standard Specification for Labeling of Plastics Designed to be Aerobically Composted in Municipal or Industrial Facilities (D6400) or its successor;</p>
          <p>(2) meets, and is labeled to reflect that it meets, the American Society for Testing and Materials Standard Specification for Labeling of End Items that Incorporate Plastics and Polymers as Coatings or Additives with Paper and Other Substrates Designed to be Aerobically Composted in Municipal or Industrial Facilities (D6868) or its successor;</p>
          <p>(3) is comprised of only wood without any coatings or additives; or</p>
          <p>(4) is comprised of only paper without any coatings or additives.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1441.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Composting.</span></h2>
          <p>"Composting" means the controlled microbial degradation of source-separated compostable materials to yield a humus-like product.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1441.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Composting rate.</span></h2>
          <p>"Composting rate" means the amount of compostable covered material that is managed through composting, divided by the total amount of compostable covered material sold or distributed into the state by the relevant unit of measurement established in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.1451">115A.1451</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1441.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Covered entity.</span></h2>
          <p>"Covered entity" means a person or location that receives covered services for covered materials in accordance with the requirements of sections 115A.144 to 115A.1463, including:</p>
          <p>(1) a single-family residence;</p>
          <p>(2) a multifamily residence;</p>
          <p>(3) a school as defined in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/120A.22#stat.120A.22.4">120A.22, subdivision 4</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/136A.62#stat.136A.62.3">136A.62, subdivision 3</a>, clauses (1) and (2); a nonpublic school as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.41#stat.123B.41.9">123B.41, subdivision 9</a>; postsecondary educational systems as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/142E.01#stat.142E.01.20">142E.01, subdivision 20</a>; a provider as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/142E.01#stat.142E.01.20">142E.01, subdivision 20</a>; and any other location where education or child care is provided;</p>
          <p>(4) a nonprofit corporation with annual revenue of less than $35,000,000; and</p>
          <p>(5) a state agency, political subdivision, public area, public entity as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.151">115A.151</a>, or other governmental unit.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1441.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Covered material.</span></h2>
          <p>"Covered material" means packaging and paper products introduced. Covered material does not include exempt materials.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1441.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Covered materials type.</span></h2>
          <p>"Covered materials type" means a singular and specific type of covered material, such as paper, plastic, metal, or glass, that:</p>
          <p>(1) can be categorized based on distinguishing chemical or physical properties, including properties that allow a covered materials type to be aggregated into a discrete commodity category for purposes of reuse, recycling, or composting; and</p>
          <p>(2) is based on similar uses in the form of a product or package.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1441.12">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.<span class="headnote">Covered services.</span></h2>
          <p>"Covered services" means collecting, transferring, transporting, sorting, processing, recovering, preparing, or otherwise managing for purposes of waste reduction, reuse, recycling, or composting. Covered services does not mean any management method according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.02">115A.02</a>, paragraph (b), clauses (4) to (6).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1441.13">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13.<span class="headnote">De minimis producer.</span></h2>
          <p>"De minimis producer" means a person that in their most recent fiscal year:</p>
          <p>(1) introduced less than one ton of covered material into this state; or</p>
          <p>(2) earned global gross revenues of less than $2,000,000.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1441.14">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 14.<span class="headnote">Drop-off collection site.</span></h2>
          <p>"Drop-off collection site" means a physical location where covered materials are accepted from the public and that is open a minimum of 12 hours weekly throughout the year.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1441.15">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 15.<span class="headnote">Environmental impact.</span></h2>
          <p>"Environmental impact" means the impact of a covered material on human health and the environment from extraction and processing of the raw materials composing the material through manufacturing; distribution; use; recovery for reuse, recycling, or composting; and final disposal.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1441.16">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 16.<span class="headnote">Exempt materials.</span></h2>
          <p>"Exempt materials" means materials, or any portion of materials, that:</p>
          <p>(1) are packaging for infant formula, as defined in United States Code, title 21, section 321(z);</p>
          <p>(2) are packaging for medical food, as defined in United States Code, title 21, section 360ee(b)(3);</p>
          <p>(3) are packaging for a fortified oral nutritional supplement used by persons who require supplemental or sole source nutrition to meet nutritional needs due to special dietary needs directly related to cancer, chronic kidney disease, diabetes, malnutrition, or failure to thrive, as those terms are defined by the International Classification of Diseases, Tenth Revision;</p>
          <p>(4) are packaging for a product regulated as a drug or medical device by the United States Food and Drug Administration, including associated components and consumable medical equipment;</p>
          <p>(5) are packaging for a medical equipment or product used in medical settings that is regulated by the United States Food and Drug Administration, including associated components and consumable medical equipment;</p>
          <p>(6) are drugs, biological products, parasiticides, medical devices, or in vitro diagnostics that are used to treat, or that are administered to, animals and are regulated by the United States Food and Drug Administration under the Federal Food, Drug, and Cosmetic Act, United States Code, title 21, section 301 et seq., by the United States Department of Agriculture under the federal Virus-Serum-Toxin Act, United States Code, title 21, section 151 et seq.;</p>
          <p>(7) are packaging for products regulated by the United States Environmental Protection Agency under the Federal Insecticide, Fungicide, and Rodenticide Act, United States Code, title 7, section 136 et seq.;</p>
          <p>(8) are packaging used to contain liquefied petroleum gas and are designed to be refilled;</p>
          <p>(9) are paper products used for a newspaper's print publications, including supplements or enclosures, that include content derived from primary sources related to news and current events;</p>
          <p>(10) are paper products used for a magazine's print publication that has a circulation of less than 95,000 and that primarily includes content derived from primary sources related to news and current events;</p>
          <p>(11) are packaging used to contain hazardous or flammable products regulated by the 2012 federal Occupational Safety and Health Administration Hazard Communication Standard, Code of Federal Regulations, title 29, section 1910.1200, that prevent the packaging from being waste reduced or made reusable, recyclable, or compostable, as determined by the commissioner;</p>
          <p>(12) are packaging that is being collected and properly managed through a paint stewardship plan approved under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.1415">115A.1415</a>;</p>
          <p>(13) are exempt materials, as determined by the commissioner under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.1453">115A.1453</a>, subdivision 6; or</p>
          <p>(14) are covered materials that:</p>
          <p>(i) a producer distributes to another producer;</p>
          <p>(ii) are subsequently used to contain a product, and the product is distributed to a commercial or business entity for the production of another product; and</p>
          <p>(iii) are not introduced to a person other than the commercial or business entity that first received the product used for the production of another product.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1441.17">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 17.<span class="headnote">Food packaging.</span></h2>
          <p>"Food packaging" has the meaning given in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/325F.075">325F.075</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1441.18">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 18.<span class="headnote">Independent auditor.</span></h2>
          <p>"Independent auditor" means an independent and actively licensed certified public accountant that is:</p>
          <p>(1) retained by a producer responsibility organization;</p>
          <p>(2) not otherwise employed by or affiliated with a producer responsibility organization; and</p>
          <p>(3) qualified to conduct an audit under state law.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1441.19">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 19.<span class="headnote">Infrastructure investment.</span></h2>
          <p>"Infrastructure investment" means an investment by a producer responsibility organization that funds or reimburses a person for:</p>
          <p>(1) equipment or facilities in which covered materials are prepared for reuse, recycling, or composting;</p>
          <p>(2) equipment or facilities used for waste reduction, reuse, recycling, or composting of covered materials; or</p>
          <p>(3) the expansion or strengthening of demand for and use of covered materials by responsible markets in the state or region.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1441.20">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 20.<span class="headnote">Introduce.</span></h2>
          <p>"Introduce" means to sell, offer for sale, distribute, or use to ship a product within or into this state.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1441.21">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 21.<span class="headnote">Living wage.</span></h2>
          <p>"Living wage" means the minimum hourly wage necessary to allow a person working 40 hours per week to afford basic needs.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1441.22">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 22.<span class="headnote">Needs assessment.</span></h2>
          <p>"Needs assessment" means an assessment conducted according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.1450#stat.115A.1450.4">115A.1450, subdivision 4</a>. Except where the context requires otherwise, needs assessment means the most recently completed needs assessment.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1441.23">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 23.<span class="headnote">Packaging.</span></h2>
          <p>"Packaging" has the meaning given in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.03">115A.03</a> and includes food packaging. Packaging does not include exempt materials.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1441.24">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 24.<span class="headnote">Paper product.</span></h2>
          <p>"Paper product" means a product made primarily from wood pulp or other cellulosic fibers but does not include bound books or products that recycling or composting facilities will not accept because of the unsafe or unsanitary nature of the paper product. Paper product does not include exempt materials.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1441.25">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 25.<span class="headnote">Postconsumer recycled content.</span></h2>
          <p>"Postconsumer recycled content" means the amount of postconsumer material used by a producer in the production of a covered materials type, divided by the total amount of that covered materials type used for products sold or distributed by the producer in that same calendar year.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1441.26">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 26.<span class="headnote">Producer.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) "Producer" means the following person responsible for compliance with requirements under sections 115A.144 to 115A.1463 for a covered material introduced:</p>
          <p>(1) for items sold in or with packaging at a physical retail location in this state:</p>
          <p>(i) if the item is sold in or with packaging under the brand of the item manufacturer or is sold in packaging that lacks identification of a brand, the producer is the person that manufactures the item;</p>
          <p>(ii) if there is no person to which item (i) applies, the producer is the person that is licensed to manufacture and sell or offer for sale to consumers in this state an item with packaging under the brand or trademark of another manufacturer or person;</p>
          <p>(iii) if there is no person to which item (i) or (ii) applies, the producer is the brand owner of the item;</p>
          <p>(iv) if there is no person described in item (i), (ii), or (iii) within the United States, the producer is the person who is the importer of record for the item into the United States for use in a commercial enterprise that sells, offers for sale, or distributes the item in this state; or</p>
          <p>(v) if there is no person described in items (i) to (iv), the producer is the person that first distributes the item in or into this state;</p>
          <p>(2) for items sold or distributed in packaging in or into this state via e-commerce, remote sale, or distribution:</p>
          <p>(i) for packaging used to directly protect or contain the item, the producer of the packaging is the same as the producer identified under clause (1); and</p>
          <p>(ii) for packaging used to ship the item to a consumer, the producer of the packaging is the person that packages the item to be shipped to the consumer;</p>
          <p>(3) for packaging that is a covered material and is not included in clauses (1) and (2), the producer of the packaging is the person that first distributes the item in or into this state;</p>
          <p>(4) for paper products that are magazines, catalogs, telephone directories, or similar publications, the producer is the publisher;</p>
          <p>(5) for paper products not described in clause (4):</p>
          <p>(i) if the paper product is sold under the manufacturer's own brand, the producer is the person that manufactures the paper product;</p>
          <p>(ii) if there is no person to which item (i) applies, the producer is the person that is the owner or licensee of a brand or trademark under which the paper product is used in a commercial enterprise, sold, offered for sale, or distributed in or into this state, whether or not the trademark is registered in this state;</p>
          <p>(iii) if there is no person to which item (i) or (ii) applies, the producer is the brand owner of the paper product;</p>
          <p>(iv) if there is no person described in item (i), (ii), or (iii) within the United States, the producer is the person that imports the paper product into the United States for use in a commercial enterprise that sells, offers for sale, or distributes the paper product in this state; or</p>
          <p>(v) if there is no person described in items (i) to (iv), the producer is the person that first distributes the paper product in or into this state; and</p>
          <p>(6) a person is the producer of a covered material sold, offered for sale, or distributed in or into this state, as defined in clauses (1) to (5), except:</p>
          <p>(i) where another person has mutually signed an agreement with a producer as defined in clauses (1) to (5) that contractually assigns responsibility to the person as the producer, and the person has joined a registered producer responsibility organization as the responsible producer for that covered material under sections 115A.144 to 115A.1463. In the event that another person is assigned responsibility as the producer under this subdivision, the producer under clauses (1) to (5) must provide written certification of that contractual agreement to the producer responsibility organization; and</p>
          <p>(ii) if the producer described in clauses (1) to (5) is a business operated wholly or in part as a franchise, the producer is the franchisor if that franchisor has franchisees that have a commercial presence within the state.</p>
          <p>(b) "Producer" does not include:</p>
          <p>(1) a state, a federal or state agency, a political subdivision, or other governmental unit;</p>
          <p>(2) a registered 501(c)(3) charitable organization or 501(c)(4) social welfare organization;</p>
          <p>(3) a de minimis producer;</p>
          <p>(4) a mill that uses any virgin wood fiber in the products it produces; or</p>
          <p>(5) a paper mill that produces container board derived from 100 percent postconsumer recycled content and nonpostconsumer recycled content.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1441.27">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 27.<span class="headnote">Producer responsibility organization.</span></h2>
          <p>"Producer responsibility organization" means a nonprofit corporation that is tax exempt under chapter 501(c)(3) of the federal Internal Revenue Code and that is created by a group of producers to implement activities under sections 115A.144 to 115A.1463.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1441.28">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 28.<span class="headnote">Recycling.</span></h2>
          <p>"Recycling" has the meaning given in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.03">115A.03</a> except that recycling does not include reuse or composting, as defined in sections 115A.144 to 115A.1463.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1441.29">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 29.<span class="headnote">Recycling rate.</span></h2>
          <p>"Recycling rate" means the amount of recyclable covered material, in aggregate or by individual covered materials type, recycled in a calendar year divided by the total amount of recyclable covered materials sold or distributed into the state by the relevant unit of measurement established in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.1451">115A.1451</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1441.30">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 30.<span class="headnote">Refill.</span></h2>
          <p>"Refill" means the continued use of a covered material by a consumer through a system that is:</p>
          <p>(1) intentionally designed and marketed for repeated filling of a covered material to reduce demand for new production of the covered material;</p>
          <p>(2) supported by adequate logistics and infrastructure to provide convenient access for consumers; and</p>
          <p>(3) compliant with all applicable federal, state, and local statutes, rules, ordinances, and other laws governing health and safety.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1441.31">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 31.<span class="headnote">Responsible market.</span></h2>
          <p>"Responsible market" means a materials market that:</p>
          <p>(1) reuses, recycles, composts, or otherwise recovers materials and disposes of contaminants in a manner that protects the environment and minimizes risks to public health and worker health and safety;</p>
          <p>(2) complies with all applicable federal, state, and local statutes, rules, ordinances, and other laws governing environmental, health, safety, and financial responsibility;</p>
          <p>(3) possesses all requisite licenses and permits required by a federal or state agency or political subdivision;</p>
          <p>(4) if the market operates in the state, manages waste according to the waste management goal and priority order of waste management practices stated in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.02">115A.02</a>; and</p>
          <p>(5) minimizes adverse impacts to environmental justice areas, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.03">115A.03</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1441.32">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 32.<span class="headnote">Return rate.</span></h2>
          <p>"Return rate" means the amount of reusable covered material in aggregate or by individual covered materials type, collected for reuse by a producer or service provider in a calendar year, divided by the total amount of reusable covered materials sold or distributed into the state by the relevant unit of measurement established in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.1451">115A.1451</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1441.33">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 33.<span class="headnote">Reusable.</span></h2>
          <p>"Reusable" means capable of reuse.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1441.34">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 34.<span class="headnote">Reuse.</span></h2>
          <p>"Reuse" means the return of a covered material to the marketplace and the continued use of the covered material by a producer or service provider when the covered material is:</p>
          <p>(1) intentionally designed and marketed to be used multiple times for its original intended purpose without a change in form;</p>
          <p>(2) designed for durability and maintenance to extend its useful life and reduce demand for new production of the covered material;</p>
          <p>(3) supported by adequate logistics and infrastructure at a retail location, by a service provider, or on behalf of or by a producer, that provides convenient access for consumers; and</p>
          <p>(4) compliant with all applicable federal, state, and local statutes, rules, ordinances, and other laws governing health and safety.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1441.35">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 35.<span class="headnote">Reuse rate.</span></h2>
          <p>"Reuse rate" means the share of units of a reusable covered material sold or distributed into the state in a calendar year that are demonstrated and deemed reusable in accordance with an approved stewardship plan under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.1451">115A.1451</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1441.36">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 36.<span class="headnote">Service provider.</span></h2>
          <p>"Service provider" means an entity that provides covered services for covered materials. A political subdivision that provides or that contracts or otherwise arranges with another party to provide covered services for covered materials within its jurisdiction may be a service provider regardless of whether it provided, contracted for, or otherwise arranged for similar services before the approval of the applicable stewardship plan.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1441.37">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 37.<span class="headnote">Third-party certification.</span></h2>
          <p>"Third-party certification" means certification by an accredited independent organization that a standard or process required by sections 115A.144 to 115A.1463, or by a stewardship plan approved under sections 115A.144 to 115A.1463, has been achieved.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1441.38">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 38.<span class="headnote">This act.</span></h2>
          <p>"This act" means sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.144">115A.144</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.1463">115A.1463</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1441.39">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 39.<span class="headnote">Toxic substance.</span></h2>
          <p>"Toxic substance" means hazardous waste, a problem material, a chemical or chemical class regulated under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.965">115A.965</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/116.943">116.943</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/325F.075">325F.075</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/325F.172">325F.172</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/325F.179">325F.179</a>, or a chemical of high concern identified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/116.9402">116.9402</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1441.40">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 40.<span class="headnote">Waste reduction or source reduction.</span></h2>
          <p>"Waste reduction" or "source reduction" has the meaning given in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.03">115A.03</a>, except that waste reduction or source reduction does not include reuse, but does include refill, as defined in sections 115A.144 to 115A.1463.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115A.1441.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/80/#laws.5.7.0">2024 c 80 art 5 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/116/#laws.5.2.0">2024 c 116 art 5 s 2</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115A.1442">
        <h1 class="shn">115A.1442 ESTABLISHMENT OF PROGRAM.</h1>
        <p>Producers must implement and finance a statewide program for packaging and paper products in accordance with sections 115A.144 to 115A.1463 that encourages redesign to reduce the environmental impacts and human health impacts and that reduces generation of covered materials waste through waste reduction, reuse, recycling, and composting and by providing for the collection, transportation, and processing of used covered materials for reuse, recycling, and composting.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115A.1442.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/116/#laws.5.3.0">2024 c 116 art 5 s 3</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115A.1443">
        <h1 class="shn">115A.1443 REGISTRATION OF PRODUCER RESPONSIBILITY ORGANIZATIONS AND SERVICE PROVIDERS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1443.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Annual registration.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) By January 1, 2025, producers must appoint a producer responsibility organization. The producer responsibility organization must register with the commissioner by July 1, 2026, and each January 1 thereafter by submitting the following:</p>
          <p>(1) contact information for a person responsible for implementing an approved stewardship plan;</p>
          <p>(2) a list of all member producers that have entered into written agreements to operate under an approved stewardship plan administered by the producer responsibility organization and, for each producer, a list of all brands of the producer's covered materials introduced;</p>
          <p>(3) copies of written agreements with each producer stating that the producer agrees to operate under an approved stewardship plan administered by the producer responsibility organization;</p>
          <p>(4) a list of current board members and the executive director if different from the person responsible for implementing an approved stewardship plan; and</p>
          <p>(5) documentation demonstrating adequate financial responsibility and financial controls to ensure proper management of funds and payment of the registration fee required under subdivision 2.</p>
          <p>(b) Following the approval of the initial producer responsibility organization and the initial stewardship plan, if more than a single producer responsibility organization is established, the producers and producer responsibility organizations must establish a coordinating body and process to prevent redundancy. The coordinating body must integrate:</p>
          <p>(1) stewardship plans of all producer responsibility organizations into a single stewardship plan that implements all requirements of sections 115A.144 to 115A.1463 and encompasses all producers when submitted to the commissioner for approval; and</p>
          <p>(2) annual reports of all producer responsibility organizations into a single annual report that covers all requirements of sections 115A.144 to 115A.1463 and encompasses all producers when submitted to the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1443.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Registration fee.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Beginning January 1, 2029, as part of its annual registration with the commissioner, a producer responsibility organization must submit to the commissioner a registration fee, as determined by the commissioner. By October 1, 2028, and annually thereafter, the commissioner must provide written notice to registered producer responsibility organizations in writing of the amount of the registration fee. If there is more than one registered producer responsibility organization, the coordinating body described in subdivision 1, paragraph (b), must equitably apportion payment of the registration fee between all registered producer responsibility organizations. The registration fee must be set at an amount anticipated to in the aggregate meet but not exceed the commissioner's estimate of the costs required to perform the commissioner's duties as described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.1445">115A.1445</a> and to otherwise administer, implement, and enforce sections 115A.144 to 115A.1463.</p>
          <p>(b) The commissioner must annually reconcile the fees paid by a producer responsibility organization under this subdivision with the actual costs incurred by the agency by means of credits or refunds to or additional payments required of a producer responsibility organization, as applicable.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1443.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Initial producer responsibility organization registration; implementation fee.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) By January 1, 2025, producers must appoint a producer responsibility organization. The producer responsibility organization must register by January 1, 2025, with the commissioner by submitting the following:</p>
          <p>(1) contact information for a person responsible for implementing an approved stewardship plan;</p>
          <p>(2) a list of current member producers that have entered into written agreements to operate under an approved stewardship plan administered by the producer responsibility organization;</p>
          <p>(3) a plan for recruiting additional member producers and executing written agreements confirming producers will operate under an approved stewardship plan administered by the producer responsibility organization;</p>
          <p>(4) a list of current board members and the executive director if different than the person responsible for implementing approved stewardship plans; and</p>
          <p>(5) documentation demonstrating adequate financial responsibility and financial controls to ensure proper management of funds and payment of the implementation fee required under paragraph (c).</p>
          <p>(b) Notwithstanding the other provisions of this section, the commissioner may not allow registration of more than one producer responsibility organization under this section before the first stewardship plan approved by the commissioner expires. If more than one producer responsibility organization applies to register under this section before the first stewardship plan is approved by the commissioner, the commissioner must select the producer responsibility organization that will represent producers until the first stewardship plan expires and, if applicable, must return the fee paid by applicants who are not selected. When selecting a producer responsibility organization, the commissioner must consider whether the producer responsibility organization:</p>
          <p>(1) has a governing board consisting of producers that represent a diversity of covered materials introduced; and</p>
          <p>(2) demonstrates adequate financial responsibility and financial controls to ensure proper management of funds.</p>
          <p>(c) By February 15, 2025, and annually until February 15, 2028, the commissioner must provide written notice to the producer responsibility organization of the commissioner's estimates of the cost required to perform the commissioner's duties as described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.1445">115A.1445</a>. The producer responsibility organization must remit payment in full for these costs to the commissioner within 45 days of receipt of this notice. The producer responsibility organization may charge each member producer a fee according to each producer's unit-, weight-, volume-, or sales-based market share or by another method it determines to be an equitable determination of each producer's payment obligation, so that the aggregate fees charged to member producers is sufficient to pay the commissioner's estimated costs in full.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1443.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Requirement for additional producer responsibility organizations.</span></h2>
          <p>After the first stewardship plan approved by the commissioner expires, the commissioner may allow registration of more than one producer responsibility organization if:</p>
          <p>(1) producers of a covered materials type or a specific covered material appoint a producer responsibility organization; or</p>
          <p>(2) producers organize under additional producer responsibility organizations.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1443.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Registration of service providers.</span></h2>
          <p>By January 1, 2025, and annually thereafter, a service provider seeking reimbursement for services provided under an approved stewardship plan according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.1451">115A.1451</a> must register with the commissioner by submitting the following information:</p>
          <p>(1) the contact information for a person representing the service provider;</p>
          <p>(2) the address of the service provider; and</p>
          <p>(3) if applicable to services provided, a report of the total amount billed for collection for covered entities, processing services, and transfer station operations provided during the preceding calendar year and, when possible, values must be separated for collection, transfer, and processing.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1443.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Disposition of fees.</span></h2>
          <p>All fees received under this section must be deposited in the state treasury and credited to the product stewardship account under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.1463">115A.1463</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115A.1443.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/116/#laws.5.4.0">2024 c 116 art 5 s 4</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115A.1444">
        <h1 class="shn">115A.1444 ESTABLISHMENT OF PRODUCER RESPONSIBILITY ADVISORY BOARD.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1444.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Establishment.</span></h2>
          <p>The Producer Responsibility Advisory Board is established to review all activities conducted by producer responsibility organizations under sections 115A.144 to 115A.1463 and to advise the commissioner and producer responsibility organizations regarding the implementation of sections 115A.144 to 115A.1463.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1444.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Membership.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) By January 1, 2025, the commissioner must establish and appoint the initial membership of the advisory board. The membership of the board must consist of the following:</p>
          <p>(1) two members representing manufacturers of covered materials or a statewide or national trade association representing those manufacturers;</p>
          <p>(2) two members representing recycling facilities that manage covered materials;</p>
          <p>(3) one member representing a waste hauler or a statewide association representing waste haulers;</p>
          <p>(4) one member representing retailers of covered materials or a statewide trade association representing those retailers;</p>
          <p>(5) one member representing a statewide nonprofit environmental organization;</p>
          <p>(6) one member representing a community-based nonprofit environmental justice organization;</p>
          <p>(7) one member representing a waste facility that receives and sorts covered materials and transfers them to another facility for reuse, recycling, or composting;</p>
          <p>(8) one member representing a waste facility that receives compostable materials for composting or a statewide trade association that represents such facilities;</p>
          <p>(9) two members representing an entity that develops or offers for sale covered materials that are designed for reuse or refill and maintained through a reuse or refill system or infrastructure or a statewide or national trade association that represents such entities;</p>
          <p>(10) three members representing organizations of political subdivisions, with at least one member representing a political subdivision outside the metropolitan area;</p>
          <p>(11) two members representing other interested parties or additional members of interests represented under clauses (1) to (10) as determined by the commissioner; and</p>
          <p>(12) one member representing the commissioner.</p>
          <p>(b) In making appointments under paragraph (a), the commissioner:</p>
          <p>(1) may not appoint members who are state legislators or registered lobbyists;</p>
          <p>(2) may not appoint members who are employees of a producer required to be members of a producer responsibility organization in this state under sections 115A.144 to 115A.1463; and</p>
          <p>(3) must endeavor to appoint members from all regions of the state.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1444.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Terms; removal.</span></h2>
          <p>A member of the advisory board appointed under subdivision 2, paragraph (a), clause (12), serves at the pleasure of the commissioner. All other members serve for a term of four years, except that the initial term for nine of the initial appointees must be two years so that membership terms are staggered. Members may be reappointed but may not serve more than eight consecutive years. The removal of members and filling of vacancies is governed by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/15.059#stat.15.059.4">15.059, subdivision 4</a>. Except as otherwise provided, chapter 15 does not apply to the board.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1444.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Compensation.</span></h2>
          <p>Members of the board must be compensated according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/15.059#stat.15.059.3">15.059, subdivision 3</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1444.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Quorum.</span></h2>
          <p>A majority of the voting board members constitutes a quorum. If there is a vacancy in the membership of the board, a majority of the remaining voting members of the board constitutes a quorum.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1444.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Voting.</span></h2>
          <p>Action by the advisory board requires a quorum and a majority of those present and voting. All members of the advisory board, except the member appointed under subdivision 2, paragraph (a), clause (12), are voting members of the board.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1444.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Meetings.</span></h2>
          <p>The advisory board must meet at least two times per year and may meet more frequently upon ten days' written notice at the request of the chair or a majority of its members.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1444.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Open meetings.</span></h2>
          <p>Meetings of the board must comply with chapter 13D.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1444.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Chair.</span></h2>
          <p>At its initial meeting, and every two years thereafter, the advisory board must elect a chair and vice-chair from among its members.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1444.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Administrative and operating support.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner must provide administrative and operating support to the advisory board, including compensation in accordance with subdivision 4, and may contract with a third-party facilitator to assist in administering the activities of the advisory board, including establishing a website or landing page on the agency website.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1444.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Conflict of interest policies.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner must assist the advisory board in developing policies and procedures governing the disclosure of actual or perceived conflicts of interest that advisory board members may have as a result of their employment or financial holdings with respect to themselves or family members. Each advisory board member is responsible for reviewing the conflict of interest policies and procedures. An advisory board member must disclose any instance of actual or perceived conflicts of interest at each meeting of the advisory board at which recommendations regarding stewardship plans, programs, operations, or activities are made by the advisory board.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115A.1444.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/116/#laws.5.5.0">2024 c 116 art 5 s 5</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115A.1445">
        <h1 class="shn">115A.1445 COMMISSIONER RESPONSIBILITIES.</h1>
        <p>The commissioner must:</p>
        <p>(1) appoint the initial membership of the advisory board by January 1, 2025, as required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.1444">115A.1444</a>;</p>
        <p>(2) provide administrative and operating support to the advisory board, as required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.1444#stat.115A.1444.10">115A.1444, subdivision 10</a>;</p>
        <p>(3) complete a preliminary assessment by December 31, 2025, and complete an initial needs assessment by December 31, 2026, and update the needs assessment every five years thereafter, as required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.1450">115A.1450</a>;</p>
        <p>(4) approve stewardship plans and amendments to stewardship plans according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.1451">115A.1451</a>;</p>
        <p>(5) provide lists established according to the requirements of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.1453">115A.1453</a> to all producer responsibility organizations by July 1, 2028;</p>
        <p>(6) establish statewide requirements as required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.1451">115A.1451, subdivision </a>7;</p>
        <p>(7) post on the agency's website:</p>
        <p>(i) the most recent registration materials submitted by producer responsibility organizations, including all information submitted under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.1443">115A.1443, subdivision </a>1, paragraph (a), clauses (1), (2), and (4);</p>
        <p>(ii) a list of registered service providers;</p>
        <p>(iii) the most recent needs assessments;</p>
        <p>(iv) any stewardship plan or amendment submitted by a producer responsibility organization under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.1451">115A.1451</a> that is in draft form during the public comment period;</p>
        <p>(v) the most recent lists established as required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.1453">115A.1453</a>;</p>
        <p>(vi) the list of exempt materials and covered materials exempt from performance targets and statewide requirements as approved in the stewardship plan;</p>
        <p>(vii) links to producer responsibility organization websites;</p>
        <p>(viii) comments of the public, advisory board, and producer responsibility organizations on the documents listed in items (iii) to (vi), and, if any, the responses of the commissioner to those comments; and</p>
        <p>(ix) links to adopted rules implementing sections 115A.144 to 115A.1463;</p>
        <p>(8) provide producer responsibility organizations with information regarding Minnesota and federal laws that prohibit toxic substances in covered materials, toxic substances' potential environmental impacts and human health impacts, and best practices to reduce intentionally added toxic substances as identified in the needs assessment;</p>
        <p>(9) approve the selection of independent auditors to perform an annual financial audit of each producer responsibility organization; and</p>
        <p>(10) consider and respond in writing to all written comments received from the advisory board.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115A.1445.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/116/#laws.5.6.0">2024 c 116 art 5 s 6</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115A.1446">
        <h1 class="shn">115A.1446 PRODUCER RESPONSIBILITY ADVISORY BOARD RESPONSIBILITIES.</h1>
        <p>The Producer Responsibility Advisory Board must:</p>
        <p>(1) convene its initial meeting by March 1, 2025;</p>
        <p>(2) consult with the commissioner regarding the scope of the needs assessments and provide written comments on needs assessments, as required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.1450">115A.1450</a>, subdivision 2;</p>
        <p>(3) advise on the development of stewardship plans and amendments to stewardship plans under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.1451">115A.1451</a>;</p>
        <p>(4) submit comments to producer responsibility organizations and to the commissioner on any matter relevant to the administration of sections 115A.144 to 115A.1463;</p>
        <p>(5) provide written comments to the commissioner during any rulemaking process undertaken by the commissioner under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.1459">115A.1459</a>; and</p>
        <p>(6) comply with all other applicable requirements of sections 115A.144 to 115A.1463.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115A.1446.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/116/#laws.5.7.0">2024 c 116 art 5 s 7</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115A.1447">
        <h1 class="shn">115A.1447 PRODUCER RESPONSIBILITY ORGANIZATION RESPONSIBILITIES.</h1>
        <p>A producer responsibility organization must:</p>
        <p>(1) register with the commissioner, as required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.1443">115A.1443</a>;</p>
        <p>(2) submit a stewardship plan to the commissioner by October 1, 2028, and every five years thereafter, as required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.1451">115A.1451</a>;</p>
        <p>(3) implement stewardship plans approved by the commissioner under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.1451">115A.1451</a>;</p>
        <p>(4) forward upon receipt from the commissioner the lists established under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.1453">115A.1453</a> to all service providers that participate in a stewardship plan administered by the producer responsibility organization;</p>
        <p>(5) collect producer fees as required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.1454">115A.1454</a>;</p>
        <p>(6) submit the reports required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.1456">115A.1456</a>;</p>
        <p>(7) ensure that producers operating under a stewardship plan administered by the producer responsibility organization comply with the requirements of the stewardship plan and with sections 115A.144 to 115A.1463;</p>
        <p>(8) expel a producer from the producer responsibility organization if efforts to return the producer to compliance with the plan or with the requirements of sections 115A.144 to 115A.1463 are unsuccessful;</p>
        <p>(9) notify the commissioner when a producer has been expelled;</p>
        <p>(10) consider and respond in writing to comments received from the advisory board, including justifications for not incorporating board recommendations;</p>
        <p>(11) provide producers with information regarding state and federal laws that prohibit substances in covered materials, including sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.965">115A.965</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/116.943">116.943</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/325F.075">325F.075</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/325F.172">325F.172 </a>to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/325F.179">325F.179</a>, and all laws prohibiting toxic substances in covered materials;</p>
        <p>(12) maintain a website under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.1457">115A.1457</a>;</p>
        <p>(13) notify the commissioner within 30 days of a change made to the contact information for a person responsible for implementing the stewardship plan, to board membership, or to the executive director;</p>
        <p>(14) assist service providers to identify and use responsible markets;</p>
        <p>(15) reimburse service providers in a timely manner using applicable reimbursement rates; and</p>
        <p>(16) comply with all other applicable requirements of sections 115A.144 to 115A.1463.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115A.1447.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/116/#laws.5.8.0">2024 c 116 art 5 s 8</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115A.1448">
        <h1 class="shn">115A.1448 PRODUCER RESPONSIBILITIES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1448.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Registration required; prohibition of sale.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) After July 1, 2025, a producer must be a member of a producer responsibility organization registered in this state.</p>
          <p>(b) After January 1, 2029, no producer may introduce covered materials, either separately or when used to package another product, unless the producer enters into a written agreement with a producer responsibility organization to operate under an approved stewardship plan.</p>
          <p>(c) After January 1, 2032, no producer may introduce covered materials unless covered services are provided for the covered materials through a program in a stewardship plan approved by the commissioner and the covered materials are:</p>
          <p>(1) reusable and capable of being managed through a reuse system that meets the reuse rate and return rate required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.1451#stat.115A.1451.7">115A.1451, subdivision 7</a>;</p>
          <p>(2) capable of refill and supported by a refill system;</p>
          <p>(3) included on the list established under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.1453#stat.115A.1453.1">115A.1453, subdivision 1</a>; or</p>
          <p>(4) included on the list established under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.1453#stat.115A.1453.2">115A.1453, subdivision 2</a>.</p>
          <p>(d) A producer responsibility organization may petition the commissioner for a two-year extension to comply with the requirements of paragraph (c). The commissioner may approve the extension if the petition demonstrates that market or technical issues prevent a specific covered material from being considered reusable or included on the lists established under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.1453">115A.1453</a>. The producer responsibility organization may petition the commissioner for additional annual extensions until January 1, 2040, if the producer responsibility organization demonstrates that market or technical issues preventing compliance persist.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.1448.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Duties.</span></h2>
