          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 14.<span class="headnote">Employees; contracts for services.</span></h2>
          <p>The district may employ persons or firms and contract for services to perform engineering, legal, or other services necessary to carry out its functions.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.69.15">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 15.<span class="headnote">Insurance.</span></h2>
          <p>The district may require any employee to obtain and file with it an individual bond or fidelity insurance policy. It may procure insurance in amounts it deems necessary to insure against liability of the board of directors and employees or both, for personal injury or death and property damage or destruction, with the force and effect stated in chapter 466, and against risks of damage to or destruction of any of its facilities, equipment, or other property as it deems necessary.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.69.16">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 16.<span class="headnote">Reviewing projects.</span></h2>
          <p>The district may require that persons shall not acquire, construct, alter, reconstruct, or operate a solid waste facility within the district without prior consultation with and approval of the district.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115A.69.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/564/#laws.8.8.0">1980 c 564 art 8 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/569/#laws.0.15.0">1982 c 569 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/213/#laws.0.3.0">1983 c 213 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/291/#laws.0.195.0">1987 c 291 s 195</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/418/#laws.2.1.0">2000 c 418 art 2 s 1</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115A.70">
        <h1 class="shn">115A.70 DESIGNATING RESOURCE RECOVERY FACILITIES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.70.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/644/#laws.0.82.0">1984 c 644 s 82</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.70.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/644/#laws.0.82.0">1984 c 644 s 82</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.70.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/644/#laws.0.82.0">1984 c 644 s 82</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.70.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/644/#laws.0.82.0">1984 c 644 s 82</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.70.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/644/#laws.0.82.0">1984 c 644 s 82</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.70.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/644/#laws.0.82.0">1984 c 644 s 82</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.70.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/644/#laws.0.82.0">1984 c 644 s 82</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.70.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Authority.</span></h2>
          <p>A waste management district possessing designation authority in its articles of incorporation may be authorized to designate a resource recovery facility under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.80">115A.80</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.89">115A.89</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115A.70.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/564/#laws.8.9.0">1980 c 564 art 8 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/569/#laws.0.16.0">1982 c 569 s 16</a>-18; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/373/#laws.0.40.0">1983 c 373 s 40</a>,41; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/644/#laws.0.34.0">1984 c 644 s 34</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115A.71">
        <h1 class="shn">115A.71 BONDING POWERS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.71.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">General.</span></h2>
          <p>A district may exercise the bonding powers provided in this section to the extent the powers are authorized by the order of the commissioner establishing the district and by its articles of incorporation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.71.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Debt.</span></h2>
          <p>The district's bonds shall be sold, issued, and secured in the manner provided in chapter 475 for revenue bonds and the district shall have the same powers and duties as a municipality and its governing body in issuing revenue bonds under that chapter. No election shall be required. The bonds may be sold at any price and at public or private sale as determined by the district and shall not be subject to any limitation as to rate.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.71.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Revenue bonds.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A district may borrow money and incur indebtedness by issuing bonds and obligations which are payable solely:</p>
          <p>(1) from revenues, income, receipts, and profits derived by the district from its operation and management of solid waste facilities;</p>
          <p>(2) from the proceeds of warrants, notes, revenue bonds, debentures, or other evidences of indebtedness issued and sold by the district which are payable solely from such revenues, income, receipts, and profits;</p>
          <p>(3) from federal or state grants, gifts, or other moneys received by the district which are available therefor.</p>
          <p>(b) Every issue of revenue bonds by the district shall be payable out of any funds or revenues from any facility of the district, subject only to agreements with the holders of particular bonds or notes pledging particular revenues or funds. If any facility of the district is funded in whole or in part by Minnesota waste management bonds issued under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.58">115A.58</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.59">115A.59</a>, the state bonds shall take priority. The district may provide for priorities of liens in the revenues between the holders of district obligations issued at different times or under different resolutions. The district may provide for the refunding of any district obligation through the issuance of other district obligations entitled to rights and priorities similar in all respects to those held by the obligations that are refunded.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115A.71.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/564/#laws.8.10.0">1980 c 564 art 8 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/335/#laws.1.269.0">1989 c 335 art 1 s 269</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/199/#laws.2.1.0">1991 c 199 art 2 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/639/#laws.5.3.0">1994 c 639 art 5 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/1/#laws.2.161.0">1Sp2005 c 1 art 2 s 161</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115A.715">
        <h1 class="shn">115A.715 SOLID WASTE AUTHORITY.</h1>
        <p>A district has all the authority of a county for solid waste management purposes that is given to counties under this chapter and chapters 400 and 473, except the authority to issue general obligation bonds or to levy property taxes. A district has the authority of a county to issue general obligation bonds and to levy property taxes only if and only to the extent that the governing body of each county that is a member of the district agrees to delegate the authority to the district. The delegation of the authority is irrevocable unless the governing body of each county that is a member of the district agrees to the revocation.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115A.715.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/337/#laws.0.29.0">1991 c 337 s 29</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115A.72">
        <h1 class="shn">115A.72 AUDIT.</h1>
        <p>The board of directors, at the close of each year's business, shall cause an audit of the books, records, and financial affairs of the district to be made by a certified public accountant or the state auditor. Copies of a written report of the audit, certified to by the auditors, shall be placed and kept on file at the principal place of business of the district and shall be filed with the secretary of state and the commissioner.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115A.72.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/564/#laws.8.11.0">1980 c 564 art 8 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/335/#laws.1.269.0">1989 c 335 art 1 s 269</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/639/#laws.5.3.0">1994 c 639 art 5 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/1/#laws.2.161.0">1Sp2005 c 1 art 2 s 161</a></p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">DESIGNATING SOLID WASTE MANAGEMENT FACILITIES</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115A.80">
        <h1 class="shn">115A.80 DESIGNATING SOLID WASTE MANAGEMENT FACILITIES; LEGISLATIVE FINDING.</h1>
        <p>In order to further the state policies and purposes expressed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.02">115A.02</a>, and to advance the public purposes served by effective solid waste management, the legislature finds and declares that it may be necessary pursuant to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.80">115A.80</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.89">115A.89</a> to authorize a qualifying solid waste management district or county to designate a solid waste processing or disposal facility.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115A.80.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/644/#laws.0.35.0">1984 c 644 s 35</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/325/#laws.0.9.0">1989 c 325 s 9</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115A.81">
        <h1 class="shn">115A.81 DEFINITIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.81.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Scope.</span></h2>
          <p>The terms used in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.80">115A.80</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.893">115A.893</a> have the meanings given them in this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.81.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Designation.</span></h2>
          <p>"Designation" means a requirement by a waste management district or county that all or any portion of the solid waste that is generated within its boundaries or any service area thereof be delivered to a processing or disposal facility identified by the district or county.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.81.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/247/#laws.2.55.0">1995 c 247 art 2 s 55</a>]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115A.81.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/644/#laws.0.36.0">1984 c 644 s 36</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/0/274/#laws.0.6.0">1985 c 274 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/348/#laws.0.23.0">1987 c 348 s 23</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/325/#laws.0.10.0">1989 c 325 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/593/#laws.1.20.0">1992 c 593 art 1 s 20</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115A.82">
        <h1 class="shn">115A.82 ELIGIBILITY.</h1>
        <p>Facilities may be designated under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.80">115A.80</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.89">115A.89</a> by:</p>
        <p>(1) a solid waste management district established pursuant to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.62">115A.62</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.72">115A.72</a> and possessing designation authority in its articles of incorporation; or</p>
        <p>(2) a county, but only for waste generated outside of the boundaries of a district qualifying under clause (1) or the Western Lake Superior Sanitary District established under chapter 458D.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115A.82.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/644/#laws.0.37.0">1984 c 644 s 37</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/464/#laws.1.16.0">1992 c 464 art 1 s 16</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115A.83">
        <h1 class="shn">115A.83 WASTES SUBJECT TO DESIGNATION; EXEMPTIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.83.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Application.</span></h2>
          <p>Designation applies to the following wastes:</p>
          <p>(1) mixed municipal solid waste; and</p>
          <p>(2) other solid waste that prior to final processing or disposal:</p>
          <p>(i) is not managed as a separate waste stream; or</p>
          <p>(ii) is managed as a separate waste stream using a waste management practice that is ranked lower on the list of waste management practices in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.02">115A.02, paragraph (b)</a>, than the primary waste management practice that would be used on the waste at the designated facility.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.83.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Exemption.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The designation may not apply to or include:</p>
          <p>(1) materials that are separated from solid waste and recovered for reuse in their original form or for use in manufacturing processes;</p>
          <p>(2) materials that are processed at a resource recovery facility at the capacity in operation at the time that the designation plan is approved by the commissioner;</p>
          <p>(3) materials that are separated at a permitted transfer station located within the boundaries of the designating authority for the purpose of recycling the materials if:</p>
          <p>(i) the transfer station was in operation on January 1, 1991; or</p>
          <p>(ii) the materials were not being separated for recycling at the designated facility at the time the transfer station began separation of the materials; or</p>
          <p>(4) recyclable materials that are being recycled, and residuals from recycling if there is at least an 85 percent volume reduction in the solid waste processed at the recycling facility and the residuals are managed as separate waste streams.</p>
          <p>(b) For the purposes of this section, "manufacturing processes" does not include the treatment of waste after collection for the purpose of composting.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115A.83.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/644/#laws.0.38.0">1984 c 644 s 38</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/325/#laws.0.11.0">1989 c 325 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/337/#laws.0.30.0">1991 c 337 s 30</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/593/#laws.1.21.0">1992 c 593 art 1 s 21</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/639/#laws.5.3.0">1994 c 639 art 5 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/1/#laws.2.161.0">1Sp2005 c 1 art 2 s 161</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115A.84">
        <h1 class="shn">115A.84 DESIGNATION PLAN.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.84.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Requirement.</span></h2>
          <p>Before commencing the designation procedure under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.85">115A.85</a>, the district or county shall adopt a comprehensive solid waste management plan or, under chapter 473, a master plan. The county or district shall then submit a plan for designation to be approved under this section. A county's or district's designation plan must be consistent with its solid waste management plan or master plan and with statewide and regional waste management goals.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.84.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Designation; plan contents.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The designation plan must evaluate:</p>
          <p>(1) the benefits of the designation, including the public purposes achieved by the conservation and recovery of resources, the furtherance of local and any district or regional waste management plans and policies, and the furtherance of the state policies and purposes expressed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.02">115A.02</a>; and</p>
          <p>(2) the estimated costs of the designation, including the direct capital, operating, and maintenance costs of the facility designated, the indirect costs, and the long-term effects of the designation.</p>
          <p>(b) In particular the designation plan must evaluate:</p>
          <p>(1) whether the designation will result in the recovery of resources or energy from materials which would otherwise be wasted;</p>
          <p>(2) whether the designation will lessen the demand for and use of indiscriminate land disposal;</p>
          <p>(3) whether the designation is necessary for the financial support of the facility;</p>
          <p>(4) whether less restrictive methods for ensuring an adequate solid waste supply are available;</p>
          <p>(5) other feasible and prudent waste management alternatives for accomplishing the purposes of the proposed designation, the direct and indirect costs of the alternatives, including capital and operating costs, and the effects of the alternatives on the cost to generators; and</p>
          <p>(6) whether the designation takes into account and promotes local, regional, and state waste management goals.</p>
          <p>(c) When the plan proposes designation to disposal facilities, the designation plan must also evaluate:</p>
          <p>(1) whether the disposal facility is part of an integrated waste management system involving a processing facility and the designation is necessary for the financial support of the processing facility;</p>
          <p>(2) whether the designation will better serve to protect public health and safety;</p>
          <p>(3) the impacts on other disposal facilities inside and outside the area;</p>
          <p>(4) whether the designation is necessary to promote regional waste management programs and cooperation; and</p>
          <p>(5) the extent to which the design and operation of the disposal facility protects the environment including whether it is permitted under current agency rules and whether any portion of the facility's site is listed under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115B.17#stat.115B.17.13">115B.17, subdivision 13</a>.</p>
          <p>(d) When the plan proposes designation to a disposal facility, mixed municipal solid waste that is subject to a contract between a hauler and a different facility that is in effect on the date notice is given under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.85#stat.115A.85.2">115A.85, subdivision 2</a>, is not subject to the designation during the contract period or for one year after the date notice is given, whichever period is shorter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.84.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Plan approval.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A district or county planning a designation shall submit the designation plan to the commissioner for review and approval or disapproval.</p>
          <p>(b) The commissioner shall complete the review and make a decision within 120 days following submission of the plan for review. The commissioner shall approve the designation plan if the plan satisfies the requirements of subdivision 2 and, in the case of designation to disposal facilities, if the commissioner finds that the plan has demonstrated that the designation is necessary and is consistent with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.02">115A.02</a>. The commissioner may attach conditions to the approval that relate to matters required in a designation ordinance under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.86#stat.115A.86.1">115A.86, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (a), clauses (1) to (4), and paragraph (b). Amendments to plans must be submitted for review in accordance with this subdivision.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.84.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Excluding certain materials.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) When the commissioner approves the designation plan, the commissioner shall exclude from the designation materials that the commissioner determines will be processed at a resource recovery facility separate from the designated facility if:</p>
          <p>(1) the resource recovery facility requesting the exclusion is substantially completed or will be substantially completed within 18 months of the time that the designation plan is approved by the commissioner;</p>
          <p>(2) the facility requesting the exclusion has or will have contracts for purchases of its product; and</p>
          <p>(3) the materials are or will be under contract for delivery to the facility requesting the exclusion at the time that facility is completed.</p>
          <p>(b) In order to qualify for the exclusion of materials under this subdivision, the operator or owner of the resource recovery facility requesting the exclusion shall file with the commissioner and the district or county or counties a written description of the facility, its intended location, its waste supply sources, purchasers of its products, its design capacity and other information that the commissioner and the district or county or counties may reasonably require. The information must be filed as soon as it becomes available but not later than 30 days following the date when the county or district submits its designation plan for approval.</p>
          <p>(c) The commissioner may revoke the exclusion granted under this subdivision when the commissioner approves the designation ordinance under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.86">115A.86</a> if in the commissioner's judgment the excluded materials will not be processed at the other facility.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.84.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Excluding materials separated at certain facilities.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A county or district shall exclude from the designation, subject to approval by the commissioner, materials that the county or district determines will be separated for recycling at a transfer station located outside of the area subject to designation if:</p>
          <p>(1) the residual materials left after separation of the recyclable materials are delivered to a facility designated by the county or district;</p>
          <p>(2) each waste collector who would otherwise be subject to the designation ordinance and who delivers waste to the transfer station has not been found in violation of the designation ordinance in the six months prior to filing for an exclusion;</p>
          <p>(3) the materials separated at the transfer station are delivered to a recycler and are actually recycled; and</p>
          <p>(4) the owner or operator of the transfer station agrees to report and actually reports to the county or district the quantities of materials, by categories to be specified by the county or district, that are recycled by the facility that otherwise would have been subject to designation.</p>
          <p>(b) In order to qualify for the exclusion in this subdivision, the owner of a transfer station shall file with the county or district a written description of the transfer station, its operation, location, and waste supply sources, the quantity of waste delivered to the transfer station by the owner of the transfer station, the market for the materials separated for recycling, where the recyclable materials are delivered for recycling, and other information the county or district may reasonably require. Information received by the county or district is nonpublic data as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/13.02#stat.13.02.9">13.02, subdivision 9</a>.</p>
          <p>(c) A county or district that grants an exclusion under this subdivision may revoke the exclusion if any of the conditions of paragraph (a) are not being met.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115A.84.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/644/#laws.0.39.0">1984 c 644 s 39</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/0/274/#laws.0.7.0">1985 c 274 s 7</a>,8; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/325/#laws.0.12.0">1989 c 325 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/335/#laws.1.269.0">1989 c 335 art 1 s 269</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/337/#laws.0.31.0">1991 c 337 s 31</a>,32; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/639/#laws.5.3.0">1994 c 639 art 5 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/247/#laws.2.18.0">1995 c 247 art 2 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/1/#laws.2.161.0">1Sp2005 c 1 art 2 s 161</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115A.85">
        <h1 class="shn">115A.85 PROCEDURE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.85.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Requirement.</span></h2>
          <p>A district or county with an approved designation plan shall proceed as provided in this section when designating facilities. A district need not repeat the designation procedures in this section to the extent that the procedures have been completed by each county having territory in the district or by a joint powers board composed of each county having territory in the district.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.85.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Hearing.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The district or county shall hold a public hearing to take testimony on the designation. Notice of the hearing must be:</p>
          <p>(1) published in a newspaper of general circulation in the area for two successive weeks ending at least 15 days before the date of the hearing; and</p>
          <p>(2) mailed to political subdivisions, processing and disposal facility operators, and licensed solid waste collectors who may be expected to use the facility.</p>
          <p>(b) The notification must:</p>
          <p>(1) describe the area in which the designation will apply and the plans for the use of the solid waste;</p>
          <p>(2) specify the point or points of delivery of the solid waste;</p>
          <p>(3) estimate the types and quantities of solid waste subject to the designation; and</p>
          <p>(4) estimate the fee to be charged for the use of the facilities and for any products of the facilities.</p>
          <p>(c) A designation or contract for use is not invalid by reason of the failure of the district or county to provide written notice to an entity listed in this subdivision.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.85.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Negotiated contracts for use.</span></h2>
          <p>During a period of 90 days following the hearing, the district or county shall negotiate with the persons entitled to written notice under subdivision 2 for the purpose of developing contractual agreements that will require use of the facilities proposed to be designated.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.85.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Designation decision.</span></h2>
          <p>At the end of the 90-day contract negotiation period, the district or county may proceed to secure approval for and implement the designation as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.86">115A.86</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115A.85.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/644/#laws.0.40.0">1984 c 644 s 40</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/325/#laws.0.13.0">1989 c 325 s 13</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115A.86">
        <h1 class="shn">115A.86 IMPLEMENTING DESIGNATION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.86.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Designation ordinance.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The district or county shall prepare a designation ordinance to implement a designation. The designation ordinance must:</p>
          <p>(1) define the geographic area and the types and quantities of solid waste subject to designation;</p>
          <p>(2) specify the point or points of delivery of the solid waste;</p>
          <p>(3) require that the designated solid waste be delivered to the specified point or points of delivery;</p>
          <p>(4) require the designated facility to accept all designated solid waste delivered to the specified point or points of delivery, unless the facility has notified waste collectors in the designated area that the facility is inoperative;</p>
          <p>(5) set out the procedures and principles to be followed by the county or district in establishing and amending any rates and charges at the designated facility; and</p>
          <p>(6) state any additional regulations governing waste collectors or other matters necessary to implement the designation.</p>
          <p>(b) The designation ordinance must provide an exception for:</p>
          <p>(1) materials that are exempt or excluded from the designation under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.83">115A.83</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.84#stat.115A.84.4">115A.84, subdivision 4</a>; and</p>
          <p>(2) materials otherwise subject to the designation for which negotiated contractual arrangements exist that will require and effect the delivery of the waste to the facility for the term of the contract.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.86.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Approval.</span></h2>
          <p>A district or county shall submit the designation ordinance, together with any negotiated contracts assuring the delivery of solid waste, to the commissioner for review and approval or disapproval. The commissioner shall complete the review and make a decision within 90 days following submission of the designation for review. The commissioner shall approve the designation if the commissioner determines that the designation procedure specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.85">115A.85</a> was followed and that the designation is based on a plan approved under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.84">115A.84</a>. The commissioner may attach conditions to the approval.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.86.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Implementation.</span></h2>
          <p>The designation may not be placed into effect before 60 days after the approval required in subdivision 2. The effective date of the designation must be specified at least 60 days in advance. If the designation is not placed into effect within two years of approval, the designation must be resubmitted to the commissioner for approval or disapproval under subdivision 2, unless bonds have been issued to finance the facility to which the designation applies.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.86.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Effect.</span></h2>
          <p>The designation is binding on all political subdivisions, landfill operators, solid waste generators, and solid waste collectors in the designation area.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.86.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Amendments.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Except for an amendment authorized under subdivision 6, amendments to a designation ordinance must be submitted to the commissioner for approval. The commissioner shall approve the amendment if the amendment is in the public interest and in furtherance of the state policies and purposes expressed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.02">115A.02</a>. If the commissioner finds that the proposed amendment is a substantive change from the existing designation plan, the commissioner may require that the county or solid waste management district submit a revised designation plan to the commissioner for approval. After receiving approval for the designation plan amendment from the commissioner, the county or district shall follow the procedure outlined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.85">115A.85</a> prior to submitting the amended designation ordinance to the commissioner for approval. If the commissioner does not act within 90 days after receiving the proposed amendment to the designation ordinance, the amendment is approved.</p>
          <p>(b) Except for an amendment authorized under subdivision 6, prior to amending an ordinance to designate solid waste to a disposal facility, a county or district shall submit an amended designation plan to the commissioner for approval, and shall follow the procedures outlined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.85">115A.85</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.86.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Penalties.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A county may include in its designation ordinance civil and misdemeanor penalties for violation of the ordinance. Subdivision 5 does not govern a designation ordinance amendment adopted under this paragraph.</p>
          <p>(b) A county may by ordinance impose civil and misdemeanor penalties for delivery of mixed municipal solid waste to a processing or disposal facility in the county that is not a facility designated to receive the waste under a designation ordinance adopted by another county under this section.</p>
          <p>(c) A civil penalty adopted under paragraph (a) or (b) must be payable to the county and may not exceed a fine of $10,000 per day of violation plus the cost of mitigating any damages caused by the violation and the attorney fees and court costs incurred by the county to enforce the ordinance.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115A.86.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/644/#laws.0.41.0">1984 c 644 s 41</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/0/274/#laws.0.9.0">1985 c 274 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/325/#laws.0.14.0">1989 c 325 s 14</a>,15; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/335/#laws.1.269.0">1989 c 335 art 1 s 269</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/337/#laws.0.33.0">1991 c 337 s 33</a>,34; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/639/#laws.5.3.0">1994 c 639 art 5 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/247/#laws.2.19.0">1995 c 247 art 2 s 19</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/1/#laws.2.161.0">1Sp2005 c 1 art 2 s 161</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115A.87">
        <h1 class="shn">115A.87 JUDICIAL REVIEW; ATTORNEY GENERAL TO PROVIDE COUNSEL.</h1>
        <p>(a) An action challenging a designation must be brought within 60 days of the approval of the designation by the commissioner. The action is subject to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/562.02">562.02</a>.</p>
        <p>(b) In any action challenging a designation ordinance or the implementation of a designation ordinance, the person bringing the challenge shall notify the attorney general. The attorney general may intervene in any administrative or court action to represent the state's interest in designation of solid waste, and, on request of a county whose designation ordinance has been challenged, provide legal representation for the county in any administrative or court action related to the challenge.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115A.87.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/644/#laws.0.42.0">1984 c 644 s 42</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/593/#laws.1.22.0">1992 c 593 art 1 s 22</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/585/#laws.0.14.0">1994 c 585 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/639/#laws.5.3.0">1994 c 639 art 5 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/1/#laws.2.161.0">1Sp2005 c 1 art 2 s 161</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115A.88">
        <h1 class="shn">115A.88 SERVICE GUARANTEE.</h1>
        <p>The district or county may not arbitrarily terminate, suspend, or curtail services provided to any person required by contract or designation ordinance to use designated facilities without the consent of the person or without just cause.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115A.88.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/644/#laws.0.43.0">1984 c 644 s 43</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115A.882">
        <h1 class="shn">115A.882 RECORDS; INSPECTION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.882.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Definitions.</span></h2>
          <p>For the purposes of this section:</p>
          <p>(1) "origin" means a general geographical description that at a minimum names the local governmental unit within a county from which waste was collected; and</p>
          <p>(2) "type" means a best estimate of the percentage of each truck load that consists of residential, commercial, industrial, construction, or any other general type of waste.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.882.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Records; collectors; facilities.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Each person who collects solid waste in a county in which a designation ordinance is in effect shall maintain records regarding the volume or weight, type, and origin of waste collected. Each day, a record of the origin, type, and weight of the waste collected that day and the identity of the waste facility at which that day's collected waste is deposited must be kept on the waste collection vehicle. If the waste is measured by volume at the waste facility at which it is deposited, the record may show the volume rather than the weight of the waste.</p>
          <p>(b) The owner or operator of a solid waste facility shall maintain records regarding the weight of the waste, or the volume of the waste if the waste is measured by volume; the general type or types of waste; the origin of the waste delivered to the facility; the date and time of delivery; and the name of the waste collector that delivered the waste to the facility.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.882.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Inspection.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A person authorized by a county in which a designation ordinance is effective may, anywhere in the state:</p>
          <p>(1) upon presentation of identification and without a search warrant, inspect or copy the records required to be kept on a waste collection vehicle under subdivision 2 and inspect the waste on the vehicle at the time of deposit of the waste at a facility;</p>
          <p>(2) when reasonable notice under the circumstances has been given, upon presentation of identification and without a search warrant, inspect or copy the records of an owner or operator of a solid waste facility that are required to be maintained under subdivision 2;</p>
          <p>(3) request, in writing, copies of records of a solid waste collector that indicate the type, origin, and weight or, if applicable, the volume of waste collected, the identity of the facility at which the waste was deposited, and the date of deposit at the facility; and</p>
          <p>(4) upon presentation of identification and without a search warrant, inspect or copy that portion of the business records of a waste collector necessary to comply with clause (3) at the central record-keeping location of the waste collector only if the collector fails to provide copies of the records within 15 days of receipt of a written request for them, unless the time has been extended by agreement of the parties.</p>
          <p>(b) Records or information received, inspected, or copied by a county under this section are classified as nonpublic data as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/13.02#stat.13.02.9">13.02, subdivision 9</a>, and may be used by the county solely for enforcement of a designation ordinance. A waste collector or the owner or operator of a waste facility shall maintain business records needed to comply with this section for two years.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.882.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Civil enforcement; venue.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A person who fails to comply with this section is subject to:</p>
          <p>(1) an action to compel performance or to restrain or enjoin any activity that interferes with the requirement to keep records in subdivision 2 or the requirement to allow timely entry and inspection in subdivision 3;</p>
          <p>(2) damages caused by the failure to keep records or by refusal to allow timely entry or inspection;</p>
          <p>(3) a civil penalty payable to the county seeking enforcement of up to $10,000 per day for each day of refusal to allow timely entry or inspection; or</p>
          <p>(4) any or all of the above.</p>
          <p>(b) A county in which a designation ordinance is in effect may enforce this section by commencing an action in district court in the county in which the facility is located or in the county in which the designation ordinance is in effect. The court may compel performance in any manner deemed appropriate by the court, including, but not limited to, issuance of an order to show cause, a temporary restraining order, or an injunction. In addition, the court may order payment of damages or a civil penalty or both. In an action brought by a county to enforce this section in which the county substantially prevails, the court may order payment by the defendant of the county's costs and disbursements, including reasonable attorney fees.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115A.882.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/521/#laws.0.1.0">1988 c 521 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/337/#laws.0.35.0">1991 c 337 s 35</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/585/#laws.0.15.0">1994 c 585 s 15</a>,16</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115A.89">
        <h1 class="shn">115A.89 SUPERVISING IMPLEMENTATION.</h1>
        <p>The commissioner shall:</p>
        <p>(1) require regular reports on the implementation of each designation;</p>
        <p>(2) periodically evaluate whether each designation as implemented has accomplished its purposes and whether the designation is in the public interest and in furtherance of the state policies and purposes expressed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.02">115A.02</a>; and</p>
        <p>(3) report periodically to the legislature on the commissioner's conclusions and recommendations.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115A.89.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/644/#laws.0.44.0">1984 c 644 s 44</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/639/#laws.5.3.0">1994 c 639 art 5 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/1/#laws.2.161.0">1Sp2005 c 1 art 2 s 161</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115A.893">
        <h1 class="shn">115A.893 PETITION FOR EXCLUSION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.893.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Petition for exclusion.</span></h2>
          <p>Any person proposing to own or operate a processing facility using waste materials subject to a designation ordinance may petition the waste district or county for exclusion of the materials from the designation ordinance. In order to qualify for the exclusion of materials under this section, the petitioner shall submit with the petition a written description of the proposed facility, its intended location, its waste supply sources, purchasers of its products, its design capacity, and other information that the district or county may reasonably require.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.893.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Decision.</span></h2>
          <p>The district or county, after appropriate notice and hearing, shall issue a written decision with findings of fact and conclusions on all material issues. The district or county shall grant the petition if it determines that:</p>
          <p>(1) the materials will be processed at the facility; and</p>
          <p>(2) the exclusion can be implemented without impairing the financial viability of the designated facility or impairing contractual obligations or preventing the performance of contracts by the facility owner or operator, the district or county, or users of the facility.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.893.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Appealing decision.</span></h2>
          <p>A person aggrieved by the decision of the district or county may appeal to the commissioner. The review is confined to the record. The decision of the commissioner must be based on the standards stated in this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.893.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Designation ordinance and petition amendments.</span></h2>
          <p>If the commissioner approves the petition, the designation ordinance must be amended in conformance with the decision of the commissioner. The petition may be amended during the proceedings by agreement between the petitioner and the district or county.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115A.893.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/0/274/#laws.0.10.0">1985 c 274 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/325/#laws.0.16.0">1989 c 325 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/639/#laws.5.3.0">1994 c 639 art 5 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/1/#laws.2.161.0">1Sp2005 c 1 art 2 s 161</a></p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">WASTE TIRES, BATTERIES, AND USED OIL</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115A.90">
        <h1 class="shn">115A.90 DEFINITIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.90.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Applicability.</span></h2>
          <p>The definitions in this section apply to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.90">115A.90</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.914">115A.914</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.90.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>MS 1990 [Renumbered subd 3]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.90.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Collection site.</span></h2>
          <p>"Collection site" means a permitted site, or a site exempted from permit, used for the storage of waste tires.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.90.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>MS 1990 [Renumbered subd 2]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.90.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>MS 1994 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/247/#laws.2.55.0">1995 c 247 art 2 s 55</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.90.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/685/#laws.0.44.0">1988 c 685 s 44</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.90.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Person.</span></h2>
          <p>"Person" has the meaning given in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/116.06#stat.116.06.17">116.06, subdivision 17</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.90.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Processing.</span></h2>
          <p>"Processing" means producing or manufacturing usable materials, including fuel, from waste tires including necessary incidental temporary storage activity.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.90.6a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6a.<span class="headnote">Shredder residue.</span></h2>
          <p>"Shredder residue" means the residue generated by shredding a motor vehicle, an appliance, or other source of recyclable steel after removing the reusable and recyclable materials.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.90.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Tire.</span></h2>
          <p>"Tire" means a pneumatic tire or solid tire for motor vehicles as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.011">169.011</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.90.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Tire collector.</span></h2>
          <p>"Tire collector" means a person who owns or operates a site used for the storage, collection, or deposit of more than 50 waste tires.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.90.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Tire dump.</span></h2>
          <p>"Tire dump" means an establishment, site, or place of business without a required tire collector or tire processor permit that is maintained, operated, used, or allowed to be used for storing, keeping, or depositing unprocessed waste tires.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.90.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Tire processor.</span></h2>
          <p>"Tire processor" means a person engaged in the processing of waste tires.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.90.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Waste tire.</span></h2>
          <p>"Waste tire" means a tire that is no longer suitable for its original intended purpose because of wear, damage, or defect.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115A.90.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/654/#laws.2.92.0">1984 c 654 art 2 s 92</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/685/#laws.0.45.0">1988 c 685 s 45</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/335/#laws.1.269.0">1989 c 335 art 1 s 269</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/172/#laws.0.58.0">1993 c 172 s 58</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115A.902">
        <h1 class="shn">115A.902 PERMIT; TIRE COLLECTORS AND PROCESSORS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.902.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Permit required.</span></h2>
          <p>A tire collector or tire processor with more than 500 waste tires shall obtain a permit from the agency unless exempted in subdivision 2. The agency may by rule require tire collectors or tire processors with less than 500 waste tires to obtain permits unless exempted by subdivision 2.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.902.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Exemptions.</span></h2>
          <p>A permit is not required for:</p>
          <p>(1) a retail tire seller for the retail selling site if no more than 500 waste tires are kept on the business premises;</p>
          <p>(2) an owner or operator of a tire retreading business for the business site if no more than 3,000 waste tires are kept on the business premises;</p>
          <p>(3) an owner or operator of a business who, in the ordinary course of business, removes tires from motor vehicles if no more than 500 waste tires are kept on the business premises;</p>
          <p>(4) a permitted landfill operator with less than 10,000 waste tires stored above ground at the permitted site; or</p>
          <p>(5) a person using waste tires for agricultural purposes if the waste tires are kept on the site of use.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.902.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Local authority.</span></h2>
          <p>The issuance of an agency permit does not replace a permit or license required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/400.16">400.16</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/473.811">473.811</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.902.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Permit fee.</span></h2>
          <p>The revenue from permit fees shall be credited to the general fund.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115A.902.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/654/#laws.2.93.0">1984 c 654 art 2 s 93</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/685/#laws.0.45.0">1988 c 685 s 45</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/335/#laws.1.269.0">1989 c 335 art 1 s 269</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/73/#laws.0.5.0">1999 c 73 s 5</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115A.904">
        <h1 class="shn">115A.904 LAND DISPOSAL PROHIBITED.</h1>
        <p>The disposal of waste tires in the land is prohibited after July 1, 1985, except for beneficial uses of tire-derived products designated by the commissioner. This does not prohibit the storage of unprocessed waste tires at a collection or processing facility.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115A.904.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/654/#laws.2.94.0">1984 c 654 art 2 s 94</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/1/13/#laws.0.230.0">1Sp1985 c 13 s 230</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/1/16/#laws.2.42.0">1Sp1985 c 16 art 2 s 42</a> subd 1; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/272/#laws.0.70.0">2012 c 272 s 70</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.115A.906"><b>115A.906</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/2/#laws.0.162.0">1Sp2001 c 2 s 162</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115A.908">
        <h1 class="shn">115A.908 MOTOR VEHICLE TRANSFER FEE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.908.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Fee charged.</span></h2>
          <p>A fee of $10 shall be charged on the initial registration and each subsequent transfer of title within the state, other than transfers for resale purposes, of every motor vehicle weighing more than 1,000 pounds. The fee shall be collected by the commissioner of public safety. Registration plates or certificates of title may not be issued by the commissioner of public safety for the ownership or operation of a motor vehicle subject to the transfer fee unless the fee is paid. The fee may not be charged on the transfer of:</p>
          <p>(1) previously registered vehicles if the transfer is to the same person;</p>
          <p>(2) vehicles subject to the conditions specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297A.70#stat.297A.70.2">297A.70, subdivision 2</a>; or</p>
          <p>(3) vehicles purchased in another state by a resident of another state if more than 60 days have elapsed after the date of purchase and the purchaser is transferring title to this state and has become a resident of this state after the purchase.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.908.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Deposit of revenue.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Revenue collected under this section shall be credited to the environmental fund.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.908.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/216/#laws.0.160.0">1997 c 216 s 160</a>]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115A.908.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/654/#laws.2.96.0">1984 c 654 art 2 s 96</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/1/13/#laws.0.231.0">1Sp1985 c 13 s 231</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/335/#laws.4.35.0">1989 c 335 art 4 s 35</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/172/#laws.0.59.0">1993 c 172 s 59</a>,60; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/220/#laws.0.97.0">1995 c 220 s 97</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/231/#laws.0.132.0">1999 c 231 s 132</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/418/#laws.1.44.0">2000 c 418 art 1 s 44</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/128/#laws.1.127.0">2003 c 128 art 1 s 127</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/6/#laws.2.1.0">1Sp2005 c 6 art 2 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/179/#laws.0.34.0">2008 c 179 s 34</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/101/#laws.2.109.0">2009 c 101 art 2 s 109</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/0/76/#laws.1.7.0">2011 c 76 art 1 s 7</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115A.909">
        <h1 class="shn">115A.909 SHREDDER RESIDUE; MANAGEMENT.</h1>
        <p>The commissioner, in consultation with persons who are engaged in the business of shredding motor vehicles, appliances, and other sources of recyclable steel, shall study management of shredder residue. To the extent possible under state and federal law, the commissioner shall encourage reduction in the amount of residue generated, allow beneficial use of the residue, and minimize costs of management and disposal. The commissioner shall study all reasonably ascertainable alternatives for management of the residue, including use as cover material at solid waste disposal facilities, use in manufacture of refuse-derived fuel, and any other resource recovery management technique.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115A.909.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/172/#laws.0.61.0">1993 c 172 s 61</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115A.912">
        <h1 class="shn">115A.912 WASTE TIRES; MANAGEMENT.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.912.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Purpose.</span></h2>
          <p>Money appropriated to the agency for waste tire management may be spent for regulation of permitted waste tire facilities, research and studies to determine the technical and economic feasibility of uses for tire-derived products, and public education on waste tire management.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.912.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/2/#laws.0.162.0">1Sp2001 c 2 s 162</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.912.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/2/#laws.0.162.0">1Sp2001 c 2 s 162</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.912.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Waste tire materials; prohibition.</span></h2>
          <p>Materials derived from waste tires may not be used as lightweight fill in the construction of public roads in the state unless the construction plan is prepared by a professional engineer experienced in the geotechnical field and licensed in the state of Minnesota. The plan shall include, but not be limited to, the location, duration, and length of the project, the depth of fill, the depth of cover, the size of waste tire pieces, the plan for encapsulating the waste tire pieces, and the fire protection plan. All engineering specifications must be consistent with the current lightweight tire fill engineering practices as developed for roadways by the Minnesota Department of Transportation.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115A.912.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/654/#laws.2.97.0">1984 c 654 art 2 s 97</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/685/#laws.0.14.0">1988 c 685 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/335/#laws.1.269.0">1989 c 335 art 1 s 269</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/216/#laws.0.96.0">1997 c 216 s 96</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/73/#laws.0.5.0">1999 c 73 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/2/#laws.0.126.0">1Sp2001 c 2 s 126</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/382/#laws.1.2.0">2002 c 382 art 1 s 2</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.115A.913"><b>115A.913</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/382/#laws.1.6.0">2002 c 382 art 1 s 6</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115A.914">
        <h1 class="shn">115A.914 ADMINISTRATION; COUNTY PLANNING AND ORDINANCES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.914.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Regulatory and enforcement powers.</span></h2>
          <p>For purposes of implementing and enforcing the waste tire programs in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.90">115A.90</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.914">115A.914</a>, the agency may exercise the regulatory and enforcement powers of the agency under chapters 115 and 116.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.914.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Agency rules.</span></h2>
          <p>The agency shall adopt rules for administration of waste tire collector and processor permits and waste tire collection.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.914.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">County planning; ordinances.</span></h2>
          <p>Counties shall include collection and processing of waste tires in the solid waste management plan prepared under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.42">115A.42</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.46">115A.46</a> and shall adopt ordinances under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/400.16">400.16</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/473.811">473.811</a> for management of waste tires that embody, but may be more restrictive than, agency rules.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115A.914.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/654/#laws.2.98.0">1984 c 654 art 2 s 98</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/1/13/#laws.0.232.0">1Sp1985 c 13 s 232</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/685/#laws.0.16.0">1988 c 685 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/335/#laws.1.269.0">1989 c 335 art 1 s 269</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/73/#laws.0.5.0">1999 c 73 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/2/#laws.0.127.0">1Sp2001 c 2 s 127</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115A.915">
        <h1 class="shn">115A.915 LEAD ACID BATTERIES; LAND DISPOSAL PROHIBITED.</h1>
        <p>A person may not place a lead acid battery in mixed municipal solid waste or dispose of a lead acid battery after January 1, 1988. A person who violates this section is guilty of a misdemeanor. This section may be enforced by the agency pursuant to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115.071">115.071</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/116.072">116.072</a>.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115A.915.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/348/#laws.0.24.0">1987 c 348 s 24</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/1/1/#laws.20.6.0">1Sp1989 c 1 art 20 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/347/#laws.1.18.0">1991 c 347 art 1 s 18</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115A.9152">
        <h1 class="shn">115A.9152 TRANSPORTING USED LEAD ACID BATTERIES.</h1>
        <p>(a) A person who transports used lead acid batteries from a retailer must deliver the batteries to a recycling facility.</p>
        <p>(b) A person who violates paragraph (a) is guilty of a misdemeanor. The failure to deliver each used lead acid battery to a recycler is a separate violation.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115A.9152.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/1/1/#laws.20.7.0">1Sp1989 c 1 art 20 s 7</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115A.9155">
        <h1 class="shn">115A.9155 DISPOSING OF CERTAIN DRY CELL BATTERIES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.9155.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Prohibition.</span></h2>
          <p>A person may not place in mixed municipal solid waste a dry cell battery containing mercuric oxide electrode, silver oxide electrode, nickel-cadmium, or sealed lead-acid that was purchased for use or used by a government agency, or an industrial, communications, or medical facility.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.9155.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Manufacturer responsibility.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A manufacturer of batteries subject to subdivision 1 shall:</p>
          <p>(1) ensure that a system for the proper collection, transportation, and processing of waste batteries exists for purchasers in Minnesota; and</p>
          <p>(2) clearly inform each final purchaser of the prohibition on disposal of waste batteries and of the system or systems for proper collection, transportation, and processing of waste batteries available to the purchaser.</p>
          <p>(b) To ensure that a system for the proper collection, transportation, and processing of waste batteries exists, a manufacturer shall:</p>
          <p>(1) identify collectors, transporters, and processors for the waste batteries and contract or otherwise expressly agree with a person or persons for the proper collection, transportation, and processing of the waste batteries; or</p>
          <p>(2) accept waste batteries returned to its manufacturing facility.</p>
          <p>(c) At the time of sale of a battery subject to subdivision 1, a manufacturer shall provide in a clear and conspicuous manner a telephone number that the final consumer of the battery can call to obtain information on specific procedures to follow in returning the battery for recycling or proper disposal. The manufacturer may include the telephone number and notice of return procedures on an invoice or other transaction document held by the purchaser. The manufacturer shall provide the telephone number to the commissioner of the agency.</p>
          <p>(d) A manufacturer shall ensure that the cost of proper collection, transportation, and processing of the waste batteries is included in the sales transaction or agreement between the manufacturer and any purchaser.</p>
          <p>(e) A manufacturer that has complied with this subdivision is not liable under subdivision 1 for improper disposal by a person other than the manufacturer of waste batteries.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115A.9155.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/409/#laws.0.1.0">1990 c 409 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/257/#laws.0.1.0">1991 c 257 s 1</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115A.9157">
        <h1 class="shn">115A.9157 RECHARGEABLE BATTERIES AND PRODUCTS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.9157.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Definition.</span></h2>
          <p>For the purpose of this section, "rechargeable battery" means a sealed nickel-cadmium battery, a sealed lead acid battery, or any other rechargeable battery, except a rechargeable battery governed by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.9155">115A.9155</a> or exempted by the commissioner under subdivision 9.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.9157.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Prohibition.</span></h2>
          <p>Effective August 1, 1991, a person may not place in mixed municipal solid waste a rechargeable battery, a rechargeable battery pack, a product with a nonremovable rechargeable battery, or a product powered by rechargeable batteries or rechargeable battery pack, from which all batteries or battery packs have not been removed.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.9157.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Collection and management costs.</span></h2>
          <p>A manufacturer of rechargeable batteries or products powered by rechargeable batteries is responsible for the costs of collecting and managing its waste rechargeable batteries and waste products to ensure that the batteries are not part of the solid waste stream.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.9157.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/13/#laws.2.7.0">2007 c 13 art 2 s 7</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.9157.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Collection and management programs.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) By September 20, 1995, the manufacturers or their representative organization shall implement permanent programs, based on the results of the pilot projects required in Minnesota Statutes 1994, section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.9157">115A.9157</a>, subdivision 4, that may be reasonably expected to collect 90 percent of the waste rechargeable batteries and the participating manufacturers' products powered by rechargeable batteries that are generated in the state. The batteries and products collected must be recycled or otherwise managed or disposed of properly.</p>
          <p>(b) In every odd-numbered year after 1995, each manufacturer or a representative organization shall provide information to the senate and house of representatives committees having jurisdiction over environment and natural resources and environment and natural resources finance that specifies at least the estimated amount of rechargeable batteries subject to this section sold in the state by each manufacturer and the amount of batteries each collected during the previous two years. A representative organization may report the amounts in aggregate for all the members of the organization.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.9157.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">List of participants.</span></h2>
          <p>A manufacturer or its representative organization shall inform the committees listed in subdivision 5 when they begin participating in the projects and programs and immediately if they withdraw participation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.9157.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Contracts.</span></h2>
          <p>A manufacturer or a representative organization of manufacturers may contract with the state or a political subdivision to provide collection services under this section. The manufacturer or organization shall fully reimburse the state or political subdivision for the value of any contractual services rendered under this subdivision.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.9157.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Anticompetitive conduct.</span></h2>
          <p>A manufacturer or organization of manufacturers and its officers, members, employees, and agents who participate in projects or programs to collect and properly manage waste rechargeable batteries or products powered by rechargeable batteries are immune from liability under state law relating to antitrust, restraint of trade, unfair trade practices, and other regulation of trade or commerce for activities related to the collection and management of batteries and products required under this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.115A.9157.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Exemptions.</span></h2>
          <p>To ensure that new types of batteries do not add additional hazardous or toxic materials to the mixed municipal solid waste stream, the commissioner of the agency may exempt a new type of rechargeable battery from the requirements of this section if it poses no unreasonable hazard when placed in and processed or disposed of as part of a mixed municipal solid waste.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.115A.9157.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/257/#laws.0.2.0">1991 c 257 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/593/#laws.1.23.0">1992 c 593 art 1 s 23</a>,24; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/585/#laws.0.17.0">1994 c 585 s 17</a>,18; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/470/#laws.0.27.0">1996 c 470 s 27</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/379/#laws.1.31.0">2002 c 379 art 1 s 31</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/1/#laws.2.161.0">1Sp2005 c 1 art 2 s 161</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/13/#laws.1.8.0">2007 c 13 art 1 s 8</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.115A.916">
        <h1 class="shn">115A.916 MOTOR VEHICLE FLUIDS AND FILTERS; PROHIBITIONS.</h1>
        <p>(a) A person may not knowingly place motor oil, brake fluid, power steering fluid, transmission fluid, motor oil filters, or motor vehicle antifreeze:</p>
        <p>(1) in solid waste or in a solid waste management facility other than a recycling facility or a household hazardous waste collection facility;</p>
        <p>(2) in or on the land, unless approved by the agency; or</p>
        <p>(3) in or on the waters of the state, in a subsurface sewage treatment system as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115.55">115.55</a>, or in a stormwater or wastewater collection or treatment system except as described in paragraph (c).</p>
        <p>(b) For the purposes of this section, "antifreeze" does not include small amounts of antifreeze contained in water used to flush the cooling system of a vehicle after the antifreeze has been drained and does not include deicer that has been used on the exterior of a vehicle.</p>
